var DECK_NAME={w:'Wanted Poster',t:'Town Hall'};

/* =====================================================================
   Rules engine. A game G is plain data so the host can send it to everyone.
   G.flow = money the bank has paid out minus money it took in (cash total always = start*n + flow).
   Log lines use %s for the acting seat, %0..%5 for seats and {third|second} verb forms.
   ===================================================================== */
function active(G){var a=[];G.seats.forEach(function(x,i){if(!x.out)a.push(i);});return a;}
function ownsAll(G,s,d){for(var k=0;k<DLOTS[d].length;k++)if(G.own[DLOTS[d][k]]!==s)return false;return true;}
function cntType(G,s,t){var c=0;for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(SP[p].t===t&&G.own[p]===s)c++;return c;}
function rentOf(G,p,sum){var sp=SP[p],o=G.own[p];if(o<0||G.mort[p])return 0;
  if(sp.t==='lot'){var l=G.lvl[p];return l>0?sp.rent[l]:sp.rent[0]*(ownsAll(G,o,sp.d)?2:1);}
  if(sp.t==='stage')return STAGE_RENT[cntType(G,o,'stage')];
  if(sp.t==='util')return (sum||7)*UTIL_MULT[cntType(G,o,'util')];return 0;}
function distLv(G,d){return DLOTS[d].map(function(p){return G.lvl[p];});}
function distBuilt(G,d){return DLOTS[d].some(function(p){return G.lvl[p]>0;});}
function canBuild(G,s,p){var sp=SP[p];if(!sp||sp.t!=='lot'||G.own[p]!==s||!ownsAll(G,s,sp.d))return false;if(DLOTS[sp.d].some(function(q){return G.mort[q];}))return false;
  var l=G.lvl[p];if(l>=5||l>Math.min.apply(null,distLv(G,sp.d)))return false;return G.seats[s].cash>=sp.bc;}
function buildable(G,s,p){var sp=SP[p];if(!sp||sp.t!=='lot'||G.own[p]!==s||!ownsAll(G,s,sp.d))return false;if(DLOTS[sp.d].some(function(q){return G.mort[q];}))return false;
  var l=G.lvl[p];return l<5&&l<=Math.min.apply(null,distLv(G,sp.d));}
function canSell(G,s,p){var sp=SP[p];if(!sp||sp.t!=='lot'||G.own[p]!==s||G.lvl[p]<=0)return false;return G.lvl[p]>=Math.max.apply(null,distLv(G,sp.d));}
function canMort(G,s,p){var sp=SP[p];if(!sp||!sp.price||G.own[p]!==s||G.mort[p])return false;if(sp.t==='lot'&&distBuilt(G,sp.d))return false;return true;}
function unmCost(p){return Math.ceil(SP[p].price/2*1.1);}
function canUnmort(G,s,p){return !!SP[p]&&G.own[p]===s&&!!G.mort[p]&&G.seats[s].cash>=unmCost(p);}
function liquid(G,s){var v=G.seats[s].cash;for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(G.own[p]===s){if(SP[p].t==='lot')v+=G.lvl[p]*SP[p].bc/2;if(!G.mort[p])v+=SP[p].price/2;}return v;}
function netWorth(G,s){var v=G.seats[s].cash;for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(G.own[p]===s){v+=G.mort[p]?SP[p].price/2:SP[p].price;if(SP[p].t==='lot')v+=G.lvl[p]*SP[p].bc;}return Math.round(v);}
function lotsOf(G,s){var a=[];for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(G.own[p]===s)a.push(p);return a;}
function canManage(G,s){if(!G||G.trade||s<0)return false;if(G.phase==='debt')return G.debt&&G.debt.s===s;return G.turn===s&&(G.phase==='roll'||G.phase==='end'||G.phase==='buy');}
function actor(G){if(!G||G.phase==='over'||G.phase==='setup')return -1;if(G.trade)return G.trade.to;if(G.phase==='auction')return G.auc.act[G.auc.cur];if(G.phase==='debt')return G.debt.s;return G.turn;}
function lvlName(l){return l===5?'a hotel':'a cabin';}

function Lg(ev,s,t){ev.L.push({s:s,t:t});}
function xfer(G,from,to,amt,ev){amt=Math.round(amt);if(!(amt>0))return;
  if(from>=0)G.seats[from].cash-=amt;else G.flow+=amt;
  if(to>=0)G.seats[to].cash+=amt;else G.flow-=amt;
  if(ev){if(from>=0)ev.fx.push({s:from,t:'-'+money(amt),c:'m'});if(to>=0)ev.fx.push({s:to,t:'+'+money(amt),c:'p'});}}
/* pay someone; not enough cash = the game waits while the payer raises money (or goes bust) */
function charge(G,s,to,amt,ev,why,then){amt=Math.round(amt);if(amt<=0)return true;
  if(G.seats[s].cash>=amt){xfer(G,s,to,amt,ev);return true;}
  G.debt={s:s,to:to,amt:amt,why:why||'',then:then||null};G.phase='debt';G.dl=0;
  Lg(ev,s,'%s {is|are} short '+money(amt-G.seats[s].cash)+' and must raise cash');ev.debt=1;return false;}
function finishStep(G){if(G.phase==='buy'||G.phase==='auction'||G.phase==='debt'||G.phase==='over')return;var t=G.seats[G.turn];G.phase=(G.again&&!t.jail&&!t.out)?'roll':'end';}
function moveBy(G,s,n,ev){var sd=G.seats[s],path=[],step=n>0?1:-1;
  for(var k=0;k<Math.abs(n);k++){sd.pos=(sd.pos+step+NSP)%NSP;path.push(sd.pos);
    if(step>0&&sd.pos===0){sd.laps++;xfer(G,-1,s,PAY,null);ev.fx.push({sp:0,s:s,t:'+'+money(PAY),c:'p'});Lg(ev,s,'%s {passes|pass} Payday: +'+money(PAY));}}
  if(path.length)ev.moves.push({s:s,path:path});}
function moveTo(G,s,tgt,ev){moveBy(G,s,(tgt-G.seats[s].pos+NSP)%NSP,ev);}
function toJail(G,s,ev){var sd=G.seats[s];sd.pos=JAILP;sd.jail=1;G.again=false;G.dblN=0;ev.moves.push({s:s,path:[JAILP],jump:1});ev.jail=s;}
function land(G,s,ev,ctx){
  var p=G.seats[s].pos,sp=SP[p];ev.at=p;
  if(sp.price){var o=G.own[p];
    if(o<0){G.phase='buy';G.pend={sp:p};Lg(ev,s,'%s {lands|land} on '+sp.name+' \u2013 for sale at '+money(sp.price));return;}
    if(o===s)return;
    if(G.mort[p]){Lg(ev,s,sp.name+' is mortgaged: no rent for %'+o);return;}
    var r=rentOf(G,p,ctx.sum);if(ctx.mult)r*=ctx.mult;if(ctx.uMult&&sp.t==='util')r=ctx.sum*ctx.uMult;
    Lg(ev,s,'%s {pays|pay} %'+o+' '+money(r)+' rent at '+sp.name);ev.rent={to:o,amt:r,sp:p};
    charge(G,s,o,r,ev,'rent');return;}
  if(sp.t==='card'){drawCard(G,s,sp.deck,ev,ctx);return;}
  if(p===OUTLAW){Lg(ev,s,'%s {is|are} run out of town!');toJail(G,s,ev);return;}
  if(sp.t==='tax'){Lg(ev,s,'%s {pays|pay} the '+sp.name+': '+money(sp.amt));charge(G,s,-1,sp.amt,ev,'tax');return;}
  if(p===WATER){Lg(ev,s,'%s {takes|take} a rest at the Watering Hole');}
}
function nearest(list,pos){var best=list[0],bd=99;list.forEach(function(q){var d=(q-pos+NSP)%NSP;if(d>0&&d<bd){bd=d;best=q;}});return best;}
function drawCard(G,s,dk,ev,ctx){
  var deck=G.decks[dk];if(!deck.length)return;
  var id=deck.shift(),c=CARD[id],sd=G.seats[s];ev.card={d:dk,id:id,s:s};
  Lg(ev,s,'%s {draws|draw} '+(dk==='w'?'a Wanted Poster':'a Town Hall notice')+': \u201c'+c.x+'\u201d');
  if(c.k!=='jc')deck.push(id);
  var others=active(G).filter(function(q){return q!==s;});
  switch(c.k){
    case 'cash':if(c.v>0)xfer(G,-1,s,c.v,ev);else charge(G,s,-1,-c.v,ev,'card');break;
    case 'to':moveTo(G,s,c.v,ev);land(G,s,ev,{sum:ctx.sum});break;
    case 'back':moveBy(G,s,-c.v,ev);land(G,s,ev,{sum:ctx.sum});break;
    case 'stage':moveTo(G,s,nearest(STAGE_SP,sd.pos),ev);land(G,s,ev,{sum:ctx.sum,mult:2});break;
    case 'util':moveTo(G,s,nearest(UTIL_SP,sd.pos),ev);land(G,s,ev,{sum:ctx.sum,uMult:10});break;
    case 'jail':toJail(G,s,ev);break;
    case 'jc':sd.jc.push(id);break;
    case 'payeach':var tot=c.v*others.length;
      if(sd.cash>=tot)others.forEach(function(o){xfer(G,s,o,c.v,ev);});
      else{G.debt={s:s,to:'each',per:c.v,amt:tot,why:'card',then:null};G.phase='debt';G.dl=0;ev.debt=1;Lg(ev,s,'%s {is|are} short and must raise cash');}
      break;
    case 'each':others.forEach(function(o){var a=Math.min(c.v,G.seats[o].cash);if(a>0)xfer(G,o,s,a,ev);});break;
    case 'rep':var n=0,h=0;for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(G.own[p]===s&&G.lvl[p]){if(G.lvl[p]===5)h++;else n+=G.lvl[p];}
      var amt=n*c.v+h*c.h;if(amt>0)charge(G,s,-1,amt,ev,'card');else Lg(ev,s,'No buildings yet: %s {pays|pay} nothing');break;
  }
}
function advance(G,s,n,ev){moveBy(G,s,n,ev);land(G,s,ev,{sum:G.dice[0]+G.dice[1]});finishStep(G);}
function doRoll(G,s,ev,forced){
  var d=forced&&forced.length===2?[clamp(forced[0]|0,1,6),clamp(forced[1]|0,1,6)]:[1+Math.floor(RNG()*6),1+Math.floor(RNG()*6)];
  G.dice=d;ev.dice=d;var sum=d[0]+d[1],dbl=d[0]===d[1],sd=G.seats[s];G.rolls++;
  if(sd.jail>0){
    if(dbl){sd.jail=0;G.again=false;Lg(ev,s,'%s {rolls|roll} doubles and {walks|walk} out of the Sheriff\u2019s Office');advance(G,s,sum,ev);return;}
    sd.jail++;
    if(sd.jail>3){sd.jail=0;G.again=false;Lg(ev,s,'Three tries: %s {pays|pay} the '+money(FINE)+' fine');ev.fine=1;
      if(charge(G,s,-1,FINE,ev,'fine',{move:sum}))advance(G,s,sum,ev);return;}
    Lg(ev,s,'No doubles: %s {stays|stay} locked up');G.again=false;G.phase='end';return;
  }
  if(dbl){G.dblN++;if(G.dblN>=3){Lg(ev,s,'Three doubles in a row! %s {is|are} run out of town');toJail(G,s,ev);G.phase='end';return;}}
  G.again=dbl;
  advance(G,s,sum,ev);
}
function startAuction(G,p,then){
  var order=[];for(var k=1;k<=G.n;k++){var q=(G.turn+k)%G.n;if(!G.seats[q].out)order.push(q);}
  G.auc={sp:p,high:0,hi:-1,act:order,cur:0,then:then};G.pend=null;G.phase='auction';G.dl=0;
}
function aucCheck(G,ev){var A=G.auc;if(!A.act.length||(A.act.length===1&&A.hi===A.act[0]))endAuction(G,ev);}
function endAuction(G,ev){
  var A=G.auc;G.auc=null;
  if(A.hi>=0){xfer(G,A.hi,-1,A.high,ev);G.own[A.sp]=A.hi;Lg(ev,A.hi,'%s {wins|win} '+SP[A.sp].name+' at auction for '+money(A.high));ev.won={s:A.hi,sp:A.sp,amt:A.high};}
  else Lg(ev,-1,'No bids: '+SP[A.sp].name+' stays with the bank');
  G.phase='x';
  if(G.aucQ.length){startAuction(G,G.aucQ.shift(),A.then);return;}
  if(A.then==='next')nextTurn(G,ev);else finishStep(G);
}
function endGame(G,ev,why){
  var A=active(G),best=A[0];A.forEach(function(s){if(netWorth(G,s)>netWorth(G,best))best=s;});
  G.phase='over';try{document.documentElement.classList.add('bt-over');}catch(e){}G.win=best;G.why=why;G.auc=null;G.trade=null;G.debt=null;G.dl=0;G.res=G.seats.map(function(_,i){return netWorth(G,i);});
  Lg(ev,best,why==='last'?'%s {is|are} the last tycoon standing!':'Time\u2019s up! %s {wins|win} with '+money(G.res[best])+' net worth');ev.over=1;
}
function nextTurn(G,ev){
  var A=active(G);if(A.length<=1){endGame(G,ev,'last');return;}
  var cur=G.turn,nx=cur;for(var k=1;k<=G.n;k++){var q=(cur+k)%G.n;if(!G.seats[q].out){nx=q;break;}}
  var dF=(G.first-cur+G.n)%G.n||G.n,dN=(nx-cur+G.n)%G.n||G.n;if(dF<=dN)G.round++;
  G.turn=nx;G.dblN=0;G.again=false;G.phase='roll';G.pend=null;G.turnN++;G.dl=0;G.offN=0;G.mg={m:0,u:0};
  if(G.mode==='quick'){if(G.quick==='laps'&&G.round>G.laps){endGame(G,ev,'laps');return;}if(G.quick==='clock'&&Date.now()>=G.endAt){endGame(G,ev,'clock');return;}}
  ev.turn=nx;
}
function bankrupt(G,s,ev){
  var d=G.debt,to=d?d.to:-1,sd=G.seats[s];
  for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(G.own[p]===s&&G.lvl[p]>0){xfer(G,-1,s,G.lvl[p]*SP[p].bc/2,null);G.lvl[p]=0;}
  var cred=(typeof to==='number'&&to>=0&&!G.seats[to].out)?to:-1;
  if(cred>=0){if(sd.cash>0)xfer(G,s,cred,sd.cash,ev);for(var q=0;q<NSP;q++)if(G.own[q]===s)G.own[q]=cred;G.seats[cred].jc=G.seats[cred].jc.concat(sd.jc);}
  else{if(sd.cash>0)xfer(G,s,-1,sd.cash,null);for(var r=0;r<NSP;r++)if(G.own[r]===s){G.own[r]=-1;G.mort[r]=0;G.aucQ.push(r);}sd.jc.forEach(function(id){G.decks[CARD[id].d].push(id);});}
  sd.jc=[];sd.out=true;sd.jail=0;G.debt=null;G.trade=null;ev.bust=s;
  Lg(ev,s,'%s {goes|go} bust!'+(cred>=0?' %'+cred+' takes everything':' The bank auctions the lots'));
  if(active(G).length<=1){G.aucQ=[];endGame(G,ev,'last');return;}
  G.phase='x';
  if(G.turn===s){if(G.aucQ.length)startAuction(G,G.aucQ.shift(),'next');else nextTurn(G,ev);}
  else{if(G.aucQ.length)startAuction(G,G.aucQ.shift(),'cont');else finishStep(G);}
}
function settle(G,ev){
  var d=G.debt,s=d.s;
  if(d.to==='each'){active(G).forEach(function(o){if(o!==s)xfer(G,s,o,d.per,ev);});}
  else xfer(G,s,d.to,d.amt,ev);
  G.debt=null;G.phase='x';Lg(ev,s,'%s {settles|settle} up: '+money(d.amt));
  if(d.then&&d.then.move){advance(G,s,d.then.move,ev);return;}
  finishStep(G);
}
/* trades: {from,to,give:{cash,lots,jc},get:{cash,lots,jc}}: "give" goes from -> to, "get" goes to -> from */
function normSide(x){x=x||{};var seen={},lots=[];(Array.isArray(x.lots)?x.lots:[]).forEach(function(p){p=+p;if(p>=0&&p<NSP&&SP[p]&&SP[p].price&&!seen[p]){seen[p]=1;lots.push(p);}});
  return {cash:Math.max(0,Math.round(+x.cash||0)),lots:lots,jc:Math.max(0,Math.min(2,Math.round(+x.jc||0)))};}
function tradeOk(G,tr){
  if(!tr)return false;var A=G.seats[tr.from],B=G.seats[tr.to];if(!A||!B||A.out||B.out||tr.from===tr.to)return false;
  function side(x,o){if(x.cash>G.seats[o].cash||x.jc>G.seats[o].jc.length)return false;
    return x.lots.every(function(p){return G.own[p]===o&&!(SP[p].t==='lot'&&distBuilt(G,SP[p].d));});}
  if(!side(tr.give,tr.from)||!side(tr.get,tr.to))return false;
  return (tr.give.cash+tr.give.lots.length+tr.give.jc)>0&&(tr.get.cash+tr.get.lots.length+tr.get.jc)>0;
}
function sideText(x){var a=x.lots.map(function(p){return SP[p].name;});if(x.jc)a.push(x.jc>1?x.jc+' Sheriff passes':'a Sheriff pass');if(x.cash)a.push(money(x.cash));return a.join(' + ')||'nothing';}
function execTrade(G,tr,ev){
  xfer(G,tr.from,tr.to,tr.give.cash,ev);xfer(G,tr.to,tr.from,tr.get.cash,ev);
  tr.give.lots.forEach(function(p){G.own[p]=tr.to;});tr.get.lots.forEach(function(p){G.own[p]=tr.from;});
  for(var i=0;i<tr.give.jc;i++)G.seats[tr.to].jc.push(G.seats[tr.from].jc.shift());
  for(var j=0;j<tr.get.jc;j++)G.seats[tr.from].jc.push(G.seats[tr.to].jc.shift());
  ev.deal={from:tr.from,to:tr.to,lots:tr.give.lots.concat(tr.get.lots)};
}
/* one action for seat s. Returns an event, or null when it isn't allowed */
function apply(G,s,a){
  if(!G||G.phase==='over'||G.phase==='setup'||!a||!G.seats[s]||G.seats[s].out)return null;
  var ev={kind:a.t,seat:s,fx:[],moves:[],L:[]},sd=G.seats[s],p=+a.p,sp=SP[p];
  switch(a.t){
    case 'roll':if(G.trade||G.phase!=='roll'||G.turn!==s)return null;doRoll(G,s,ev,a.d);break;
    case 'buy':if(G.trade||G.phase!=='buy'||G.turn!==s)return null;p=G.pend.sp;if(sd.cash<SP[p].price)return null;
      xfer(G,s,-1,SP[p].price,ev);G.own[p]=s;G.pend=null;ev.bought=p;Lg(ev,s,'%s {buys|buy} '+SP[p].name+' for '+money(SP[p].price));G.phase='x';finishStep(G);break;
    case 'decline':if(G.trade||G.phase!=='buy'||G.turn!==s)return null;p=G.pend.sp;Lg(ev,s,'%s {passes|pass} on '+SP[p].name+': it goes to auction');startAuction(G,p,'cont');ev.auc=p;break;
    case 'bid':if(G.phase!=='auction'||G.auc.act[G.auc.cur]!==s)return null;var amt=Math.round(+a.amt);
      if(!(amt>G.auc.high&&amt>=MINBID&&amt<=sd.cash))return null;
      G.auc.high=amt;G.auc.hi=s;G.auc.cur=(G.auc.cur+1)%G.auc.act.length;ev.bid=amt;G.dl=0;aucCheck(G,ev);break;
    case 'pass':if(G.phase!=='auction'||G.auc.act[G.auc.cur]!==s)return null;G.auc.act.splice(G.auc.cur,1);if(G.auc.cur>=G.auc.act.length)G.auc.cur=0;G.dl=0;aucCheck(G,ev);break;
    case 'end':if(G.trade||G.phase!=='end'||G.turn!==s)return null;nextTurn(G,ev);break;
    case 'build':if(G.phase==='debt'||!canManage(G,s)||!canBuild(G,s,p))return null;xfer(G,s,-1,sp.bc,ev);G.lvl[p]++;ev.sp=p;Lg(ev,s,'%s {builds|build} '+lvlName(G.lvl[p])+' on '+sp.name);break;
    case 'sell':if(!canManage(G,s)||!canSell(G,s,p))return null;G.lvl[p]--;xfer(G,-1,s,sp.bc/2,ev);ev.sp=p;Lg(ev,s,'%s {sells|sell} a building on '+sp.name+' for '+money(sp.bc/2));break;
    case 'mort':if(!canManage(G,s)||!canMort(G,s,p))return null;G.mort[p]=1;xfer(G,-1,s,sp.price/2,ev);ev.sp=p;Lg(ev,s,'%s {mortgages|mortgage} '+sp.name+' for '+money(sp.price/2));break;
    case 'unmort':if(G.phase==='debt'||!canManage(G,s)||!canUnmort(G,s,p))return null;xfer(G,s,-1,unmCost(p),ev);G.mort[p]=0;ev.sp=p;Lg(ev,s,'%s {lifts|lift} the mortgage on '+sp.name+' ('+money(unmCost(p))+')');break;
    case 'jailpay':if(G.trade||G.phase!=='roll'||G.turn!==s||!sd.jail||sd.cash<FINE)return null;xfer(G,s,-1,FINE,ev);sd.jail=0;Lg(ev,s,'%s {pays|pay} the '+money(FINE)+' fine and {walks|walk} out');break;
    case 'jailcard':if(G.trade||G.phase!=='roll'||G.turn!==s||!sd.jail||!sd.jc.length)return null;var id=sd.jc.shift();G.decks[CARD[id].d].push(id);sd.jail=0;Lg(ev,s,'%s {shows|show} a Sheriff pass and {walks|walk} out');break;
    case 'pay':if(G.phase!=='debt'||G.debt.s!==s||sd.cash<G.debt.amt)return null;settle(G,ev);break;
    case 'bankrupt':if(G.phase!=='debt'||G.debt.s!==s)return null;bankrupt(G,s,ev);break;
    case 'offer':if(G.trade||G.turn!==s||!(G.phase==='roll'||G.phase==='end')||(G.offN||0)>=4)return null;
      var tr={from:s,to:+a.to,give:normSide(a.give),get:normSide(a.get),cn:0};if(!tradeOk(G,tr))return null;
      G.trade=tr;G.offN=(G.offN||0)+1;G.dl=0;ev.offer=1;Lg(ev,s,'%s {offers|offer} %'+tr.to+' '+sideText(tr.give)+' for '+sideText(tr.get));break;
    case 'respond':if(!G.trade||G.trade.to!==s)return null;var T=G.trade;G.dl=0;
      if(a.ok){G.trade=null;if(!tradeOk(G,T)){Lg(ev,s,'The deal fell through');break;}execTrade(G,T,ev);Lg(ev,s,'%s {takes|take} the deal with %'+T.from);}
      else if(a.counter&&!T.cn){var c={from:s,to:T.from,give:normSide(a.counter.give),get:normSide(a.counter.get),cn:1};
        if(!tradeOk(G,c)){G.trade=null;Lg(ev,s,'%s {turns|turn} the deal down');break;}
        G.trade=c;ev.offer=1;Lg(ev,s,'%s {counters|counter}: '+sideText(c.give)+' for '+sideText(c.get));}
      else{G.trade=null;Lg(ev,s,'%s {turns|turn} the deal down');ev.no=1;}
      break;
    default:return null;
  }
  return ev;
}

/* =====================================================================
   Computer players
   ===================================================================== */
function oppDev(G,s){var n=0;for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(G.own[p]>=0&&G.own[p]!==s)n+=G.lvl[p];return n;}
function dangerRent(G,s){var m=0;for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++){var o=G.own[p];if(o>=0&&o!==s&&!G.mort[p]&&!G.seats[o].out){var r=SP[p].t==='util'?8*UTIL_MULT[cntType(G,o,'util')]:rentOf(G,p,7);if(r>m)m=r;}}return m;}
function cushion(G,s,skill){if(skill==='easy')return 0;var dr=dangerRent(G,s);return skill==='hard'?clamp(dr*0.7,60,650):clamp(Math.max(150,dr*0.55),150,750);}
function lotVal(G,s,p,skill){
  var sp=SP[p],m=1;
  if(sp.t==='lot'){var mine=0,free=0,oth={};DLOTS[sp.d].forEach(function(q){if(q===p)return;var o=G.own[q];if(o===s)mine++;else if(o<0)free++;else oth[o]=(oth[o]||0)+1;});
    var need=DLOTS[sp.d].length-1;if(mine===need)m+=1.2;else if(mine===1)m+=free?0.35:0.12;else if(free===need)m+=0.1;
    for(var o in oth)if(oth[o]===need)m+=skill==='hard'?0.9:skill==='normal'?0.45:0;
    if(skill==='hard')m*=0.6+0.4*FREQ[p]*NSP;}
  else if(sp.t==='stage')m=1+0.3*cntType(G,s,'stage');
  else m=0.8+0.35*cntType(G,s,'util');
  return sp.price*m;
}
function HV(G,s,skill){
  var v=G.seats[s].cash+G.seats[s].jc.length*40;
  for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++){if(G.own[p]!==s)continue;var sp=SP[p],b=sp.price*(G.mort[p]?0.55:1);
    if(sp.t==='lot'){var c=0;DLOTS[sp.d].forEach(function(q){if(G.own[q]===s)c++;});var ln=DLOTS[sp.d].length,m=c===ln?2.2:c===ln-1?1.3:1;if(skill==='hard')m*=0.7+0.3*FREQ[p]*NSP;v+=b*m+G.lvl[p]*sp.bc;}
    else if(sp.t==='stage')v+=b*(1+0.25*(cntType(G,s,'stage')-1));
    else v+=b*(cntType(G,s,'util')===2?1.2:0.9);}
  return v;
}
function simTrade(G,tr){var g={own:G.own.slice(),mort:G.mort,lvl:G.lvl,seats:G.seats.map(function(x){return {cash:x.cash,jc:x.jc.slice(),out:x.out};})};
  g.seats[tr.from].cash+=tr.get.cash-tr.give.cash;g.seats[tr.to].cash+=tr.give.cash-tr.get.cash;
  tr.give.lots.forEach(function(p){g.own[p]=tr.to;});tr.get.lots.forEach(function(p){g.own[p]=tr.from;});
  for(var i=0;i<tr.give.jc;i++)g.seats[tr.to].jc.push(g.seats[tr.from].jc.shift());for(var j=0;j<tr.get.jc;j++)g.seats[tr.from].jc.push(g.seats[tr.to].jc.shift());return g;}
/* how good a trade is for the seat that receives it (>= 0: take it) */
function tradeScore(G,tr,skill){
  var b=tr.to,f=tr.from,g=simTrade(G,tr),mine=HV(g,b,skill)-HV(G,b,skill),theirs=HV(g,f,skill)-HV(G,f,skill);
  var w=skill==='easy'?0.1:skill==='normal'?0.4:0.65,margin=skill==='easy'?-15:skill==='normal'?10:30;
  return mine-w*Math.max(0,theirs-mine)-margin;
}
function botRespond(G,s,skill){
  var tr=G.trade;if(tradeScore(G,tr,skill)>=0)return {t:'respond',ok:true};
  if(skill==='hard'&&!tr.cn){var room=G.seats[tr.from].cash-tr.give.cash;
    for(var x=10;x<=room;x+=10){var t2={from:tr.from,to:tr.to,give:{cash:tr.give.cash+x,lots:tr.give.lots,jc:tr.give.jc},get:tr.get};
      if(tradeScore(G,t2,skill)>=0)return {t:'respond',counter:{give:tr.get,get:t2.give}};}}
  return {t:'respond',ok:false};
}
function botPropose(G,s,skill){
  if((G.offN||0)>=1)return null;
  var me=G.seats[s];G.tried=G.tried||{};
  for(var d=7;d>=0;d--){
    var mine=DLOTS[d].filter(function(q){return G.own[q]===s;});if(mine.length!==DLOTS[d].length-1)continue;
    var p=DLOTS[d].filter(function(q){return G.own[q]!==s;})[0],o=G.own[p];
    if(o<0||G.seats[o].out||distBuilt(G,d))continue;
    var key=s+':'+p;if(G.tried[key]&&G.turnN-G.tried[key]<G.n*3)continue;
    var price=SP[p].price,offer=Math.round(price*(skill==='hard'?1.45:1.7)/10)*10,maxCash=me.cash-(skill==='hard'?100:160);
    var give={cash:0,lots:[],jc:0},get={cash:0,lots:[p],jc:0};
    if(maxCash>=offer)give.cash=offer;
    else if(skill==='hard'){
      var sw=null;lotsOf(G,s).forEach(function(q){var t=SP[q];if(sw||t.t!=='lot'||t.d===d||distBuilt(G,t.d))return;if(DLOTS[t.d].filter(function(x){return G.own[x]===o;}).length===DLOTS[t.d].length-1)sw=q;});
      if(!sw)lotsOf(G,s).forEach(function(q){var t=SP[q];if(sw||t.t!=='lot'||t.d===d||G.mort[q]||distBuilt(G,t.d))return;if(DLOTS[t.d].filter(function(x){return G.own[x]===s;}).length===1&&t.price>=price*0.6)sw=q;});
      if(sw===null)continue;give.lots=[sw];give.cash=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxCash,Math.round((price*1.3-SP[sw].price)/10)*10));
    } else continue;
    G.tried[key]=G.turnN;
    return {t:'offer',to:o,give:give,get:get};
  }
  return null;
}
function botBuy(G,s,skill){var p=G.pend.sp,sp=SP[p],c=G.seats[s].cash;if(c<sp.price)return false;
  if(skill==='easy')return R()<0.95;
  var lv=lotVal(G,s,p,skill),cu=cushion(G,s,skill);
  if(c-sp.price>=cu)return true;
  return lv>=sp.price*1.9&&c-sp.price>=(skill==='hard'?0:40);}
function botBid(G,s,skill){var A=G.auc,sp=SP[A.sp],c=G.seats[s].cash,max;
  if(skill==='easy')max=Math.min(c-20,sp.price*(0.5+R()*0.55));
  else{var lv=lotVal(G,s,A.sp,skill);max=Math.min(lv*(skill==='hard'?1:0.9),c-cushion(G,s,skill)*(skill==='hard'?0.5:0.8));}
  var step=A.high<100?10:A.high<400?20:50,nb=Math.max(MINBID,A.high+step);
  return nb<=max&&nb<=c?{t:'bid',amt:nb}:{t:'pass'};}
function botDebt(G,s){var d=G.debt,sd=G.seats[s];if(sd.cash>=d.amt)return {t:'pay'};
  if(liquid(G,s)<d.amt)return {t:'bankrupt'};
  var best=-1,bv=1e9,p;
  for(p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(canMort(G,s,p)){var t=SP[p],set=t.t==='lot'&&ownsAll(G,s,t.d),v=t.price*(set?4:1)*(t.t==='stage'?1.4:1);if(v<bv){bv=v;best=p;}}
  if(best>=0&&bv<1000&&!(SP[best].t==='lot'&&ownsAll(G,s,SP[best].d)))return {t:'mort',p:best};
  best=-1;bv=1e9;for(p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(canSell(G,s,p)){if(SP[p].price<bv){bv=SP[p].price;best=p;}}
  if(best>=0)return {t:'sell',p:best};
  best=-1;bv=1e9;for(p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(canMort(G,s,p)&&SP[p].price<bv){bv=SP[p].price;best=p;}
  return best>=0?{t:'mort',p:best}:{t:'bankrupt'};}
function botJail(G,s,skill){var sd=G.seats[s],late=oppDev(G,s)>=6,free=0;for(var p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(SP[p].price&&G.own[p]<0)free++;
  if(skill==='hard'&&late)return {t:'roll'};
  if(sd.jc.length)return {t:'jailcard'};
  if(skill==='easy')return sd.cash>=FINE+100?{t:'jailpay'}:{t:'roll'};
  if(sd.cash>=FINE+cushion(G,s,skill)*0.5&&(free>6||skill==='hard'))return {t:'jailpay'};
  return {t:'roll'};}
function botManage(G,s,skill){
  var sd=G.seats[s],cu=cushion(G,s,skill),p,mg=G.mg||(G.mg={m:0,u:0});
  /* lift mortgages when flush */
  if(!mg.m)for(p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(G.own[p]===s&&G.mort[p]&&canUnmort(G,s,p)){var t=SP[p],key=t.t==='stage'||(t.t==='lot'&&ownsAll(G,s,t.d));
    if(skill==='easy'?sd.cash-unmCost(p)>=700:sd.cash-unmCost(p)>=cu+(skill==='hard'?150:250)&&(key||sd.cash>900)){mg.u++;return {t:'unmort',p:p};}}
  /* build */
  if(skill==='easy'){if(R()<0.12){for(p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(canBuild(G,s,p)&&sd.cash-SP[p].bc>=300)return {t:'build',p:p};}}
  else{var best=-1,bv=-1;for(p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(canBuild(G,s,p)&&sd.cash-SP[p].bc>=cu){var l=G.lvl[p],g=(SP[p].rent[l+1]-(l?SP[p].rent[l]:SP[p].rent[0]*2))/SP[p].bc;
      if(skill==='hard'){g*=FREQ[p]*NSP;if(l+1<=3)g*=1.3;}if(g>bv){bv=g;best=p;}}
    if(best>=0)return {t:'build',p:best};}
  /* Hard: mortgage odd lots to push a district up to three cabins */
  if(skill==='hard'&&!mg.u&&mg.m<3){
    var want=false;for(var d=0;d<8;d++)if(ownsAll(G,s,d)&&!DLOTS[d].some(function(q){return G.mort[q];})&&Math.min.apply(null,distLv(G,d))<3&&sd.cash<DIST[d].bc+cu*0.5)want=true;
    if(want){var mb=-1,mv=1e9;for(p=0;p<NSP;p++)if(canMort(G,s,p)){var t2=SP[p];if(t2.t==='lot'&&DLOTS[t2.d].filter(function(q){return G.own[q]===s;}).length>1)continue;if(t2.t==='stage'&&cntType(G,s,'stage')>=2)continue;if(t2.price<mv){mv=t2.price;mb=p;}}
      if(mb>=0){mg.m++;return {t:'mort',p:mb};}}}
  /* trades to finish a district */
  if(skill!=='easy'||R()<0.05){var o=botPropose(G,s,skill);if(o)return o;}
  return null;
}
function botAction(G,s,skill){
  skill=skill||'normal';
  if(G.trade){return G.trade.to===s?botRespond(G,s,skill):null;}
  switch(G.phase){
    case 'auction':return G.auc.act[G.auc.cur]===s?botBid(G,s,skill):null;
    case 'debt':return G.debt.s===s?botDebt(G,s):null;
    case 'buy':return G.turn===s?(botBuy(G,s,skill)?{t:'buy'}:{t:'decline'}):null;
    case 'roll':if(G.turn!==s)return null;return G.seats[s].jail?botJail(G,s,skill):{t:'roll'};
    case 'end':if(G.turn!==s)return null;return botManage(G,s,skill)||{t:'end'};
  }
  return null;
}
function fallback(G,s){if(G.trade&&G.trade.to===s)return {t:'respond',ok:false};
  return {auction:{t:'pass'},debt:{t:'bankrupt'},buy:{t:'decline'},roll:{t:'roll'},end:{t:'end'}}[G.phase]||null;}

function regOf(name){for(var i=0;i<REG.length;i++)if(REG[i][0]===name)return REG[i];return null;}

function pipHTML(l){if(!l)return '';if(l===5)return '<span class="pipb h">'+(HOTEL?'<img src="'+HOTEL+'" alt="" style="width:100%;height:100%">':HOTEL_SVG)+'</span>';

function artKey(sp){return 'deed_'+String(sp.name).toLowerCase().replace(/['\u2019]/g,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'_').replace(/^_+|_+$/g,'');}
function spCol(sp){return sp.c||{stage:'#b45309',util:'#475569',tax:'#7f1d1d',card:sp.deck==='w'?'#9a3412':'#1d3d5a',corner:'#a16207'}[sp.t]||'#475569';}
function shade(hex,f){var h=hex.replace('#','');if(h.length===3)h=h.replace(/./g,'$&$&');var n=parseInt(h,16),r=n>>16&255,g=n>>8&255,b=n&255;
  function c(v){return Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(f<0?v*(1+f):v+(255-v)*f)));}return 'rgb('+c(r)+','+c(g)+','+c(b)+')';}
var LOT_ICO='<svg viewBox="0 0 40 40"><path d="M5 36V14h5V9h20v5h5v22z" fill="#f3e5c3" stroke="#2b1a0a" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="M5 14h30" stroke="#2b1a0a" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M10 9h20" stroke="#2b1a0a" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M16 36V25h8v11z" fill="#8b5a2b" stroke="#2b1a0a" stroke-width="1.8"/><path d="M8 18h6v5H8zM26 18h6v5h-6z" fill="#fde68a" stroke="#2b1a0a" stroke-width="1.6"/><path d="M3 36h34" stroke="#2b1a0a" stroke-width="2.4"/></svg>';
function spaceArtUrl(sp){if(sp.price)return DEED_ART[artKey(sp)]||'';
  if(sp.t==='card'){var base=sp.deck==='w'?'space_wanted':'space_townhall';return (sp.ord>1&&SPACE_ART[base+'_'+sp.ord])||SPACE_ART[base]||'';}
  var k=sp.t==='corner'?{P:'space_payday',J:'space_sheriff',R:'space_watering_hole',O:'space_run_out'}[sp.k]:sp.t==='tax'?(sp.amt===200?'space_claim_fee':'space_land_office'):'';
  return k?SPACE_ART[k]||'':'';}
/* put pictures on the board spaces (behind the text; lots keep their district color bar) */
function applySpaceArt(){SP.forEach(function(sp,i){var el=SPEL[i];if(!el)return;var u=spaceArtUrl(sp),a=el.querySelector('.spart');
  if(!u){if(a)a.remove();el.classList.remove('hasart');return;}
  if(!a){a=document.createElement('span');a.className='spart';var inn=el.querySelector('.in');
    if(sp.t==='lot'&&inn){['left','top','width','height'].forEach(function(k){a.style[k]=inn.style[k];});}else{a.style.left=a.style.top='0';a.style.width=a.style.height='100%';}
    el.insertBefore(a,el.firstChild);}
  a.style.backgroundImage='url(\''+String(u).replace(/'/g,'%27')+'\')';el.classList.add('hasart');});}
function spIcon(sp){if(sp.t==='lot')return LOT_ICO;if(sp.t==='stage')return ICO.stage;if(sp.t==='util')return sp.ic==='water'?ICO.water2:ICO.wire;if(sp.t==='tax')return ICO.tax;if(sp.t==='card')return sp.deck==='w'?ICO.poster:ICO.hall;
  return {P:ICO.pay,J:ICO.jail,R:ICO.water,O:ICO.out}[sp.k]||LOT_ICO;}
/* the picture at the top of a deed card: art when DEED_ART has it, otherwise a drawn scene in the district color (same 4:3 box) */
function artHTML(p,cls){var sp=SP[p],u=sp.price?DEED_ART[artKey(sp)]:'';
  if(u)return '<span class="dart'+(cls?' '+cls:'')+'" style="background-image:url(\''+String(u).replace(/'/g,'%27')+'\')"></span>';
  var c=spCol(sp);
  return '<span class="dart fb'+(cls?' '+cls:'')+'" style="background:radial-gradient(ellipse 70% 55% at 30% 18%,rgba(255,255,255,.38),transparent 60%),linear-gradient(180deg,transparent 72%,rgba(43,26,10,.38) 72.5%),linear-gradient(165deg,'+shade(c,0.25)+','+c+' 55%,'+shade(c,-0.35)+')"><span class="dic">'+spIcon(sp)+'</span></span>';}

var DIE_ON={1:[4],2:[0,8],3:[0,4,8],4:[0,2,6,8],5:[0,2,4,6,8],6:[0,2,3,5,6,8]};
function setDie(el,v){if(DICE_SHEET){el.classList.add('art');el.style.backgroundImage='url('+DICE_SHEET+')';el.style.backgroundPosition=((v-1)*20)+'% 0';el.style.backgroundSize='600% 100%';}
